## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 39, 40, 41, 126 and 174).

## Cuba added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). Into this lesson the placements stock 12 items (items 9, 21, 143, 144, 145, 156, 157, 166, 185, 186, 190, 201): 11 student items and 1 teacher-evidence item.

**Book 2:** *Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática 1* (student workbook, Grade 1) — Cuba, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, ©1988, 14th reprint 2005 (based on the former GDR Matemática 1 cuaderno); 114 PDF pp; printed page = PDF page − 3. Pupil workbook.

**Book 3:** *Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática 2* (student workbook, Grade 2) — Cuba, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, ©1989, 22nd reprint 2019, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1; 134 PDF pp; printed page = PDF page − 8. ## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 items (item 117).

**Book 5:** *Математика за 4 клас, 2 част* (pupil textbook, Grade 4, part 2) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 1st edition 2019, ISBN 978-619-7523-29-4; same authors as part 1; 20,143,337 bytes, 94 PDF pp. FULL BOOK — part 2 of 2 (lessons 63–136 in the part's own pagination, contents to printed p. 90); Part 2 restarts its own pagination (printed folio = PDF page − 1) and its citations are marked “Part 2”. Pupil textbook.

## Dominican Republic added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. Printed page = PDF page in every book (verified on the unit dividers and page footers). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught topic — a named section or a coherent teaching block inside a unit. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–14 in the placements' order. The Dominican Republic COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

Into this lesson the placements stock 15 items from Book 7 and Book 8.

**Book 7:** *Matemática 2° Secundaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 68,711,683 bytes, 208 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 8:** *Matemática 3° Secundaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 45,474,831 bytes, 208 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-textbook rows. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item (1 pupil-textbook) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Mathématiques 4e* — Collection Inter-Africaine de Mathématiques (CIAM), livre de l'élève (pupil textbook) — EDICEF (Édition 14; Dépôt Légal 41557-12/2003); 80,147,280 bytes, 224 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. Chapter pages are the sommaire's printed pages.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Chapter 10 “Calcul littéral” — literal calculation: expanding, factorising, remarkable identities | Gabon — *CIAM Mathématiques 4e* (pupil textbook), ch. 10, printed p. 129 |

Gabon is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-textbook rows. Gabon's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED for the in-hand set**, with the pupil-coverage caveat recorded above: no primary pupil book is in hand — primary coverage rests on the IPN curricula and the *Super Efficace* teacher guides.

## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1), Book 2 (TT2 GL), Book 3 (TT3), Book 4 (TT4 Eng), Book 5 (TT6).

Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 16 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 3 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 4 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 5 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 6 (*Form 3 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Mathematics for Year 11*).

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Year 3* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 37,315,079 bytes, 80 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Year 4* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 96,814,549 bytes, 86 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Folios cited as printed in the book; the folio sequence is irregular (folios 44–48 and 54 never appear, and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice) — the leaf count closes exactly and every contents strand and unit is present, so the gaps are the book's own folio misprints, not missing leaves.

**Book 3:** *Year 6 Mathematics Textbook* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit; 3,706,247 bytes, 91 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. The interior copyright/acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy error in the book itself; cover, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout.

**Book 4:** *Year 7 Mathematics Textbook* — Ministry of Education (cover: “FIJI MATHEMATICS / YEAR 7”); 6,300,620 bytes, 139 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 5 pupil-textbook rows and 3 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 13 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 7mo grado (2018)*), Book 14 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 8vo grado (2018)*), Book 15 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 7mo grado*), Book 16 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 8vo grado*), Book 17 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas and 9no grado*).

**Book 13:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 7mo grado* (2018) (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 35,622,077 bytes, 212 PDF pp. Guide printed page = PDF page − 12. Teacher evidence only.

**Book 14:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 8vo grado* (2018) (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 49,835,187 bytes, 234 PDF pp. Guide printed page = PDF page − 12. Teacher evidence only. The 2022 revised edition of this guide (a scanned book, 625,046,231 bytes, 244 PDF pp., read by OCR at cover, presentation and index level) is also held: a duplicate-edition record (placements item 338), cited once with this book, not re-placed lesson by lesson.

**Book 15:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 7mo grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 35,457,285 bytes, 164 PDF pp. Scanned-image PDF with no text layer: read by OCR of its Índice and unit/lesson openers, cross-checked against the full text-layer read of its own teacher guide (Book 13), which plans every lesson class by class. Printed page = PDF page − 4. Pupil textbook.

**Book 16:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 8vo grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 38,432,049 bytes, 184 PDF pp. Scanned-image PDF with no text layer: read by OCR of its Índice and unit/lesson openers, cross-checked against the full text-layer read of its own teacher guide (Book 14). Printed page = PDF page − 4. Pupil textbook.

**Book 17:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 9no grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; the mirror copy, 37,650,026 bytes, 190 PDF pp. (not the se.gob.hn file of the same filename, which is the Español 9 book). Scanned-image PDF with no text layer: read by OCR of its Índice and unit/lesson openers, with interior pages OCR-sampled. Printed page = PDF page − 4. Pupil textbook.

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 20 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 5 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1*) and Book 6 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1*).

**Book 5:** Sasso/Zoli, *Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1* (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola, 2017, ISBN 9788849421729; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 76,934,490 bytes, 802 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 6:** Sasso/Zanone, *Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1* (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola, 2017, ISBN 9788849421668; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 93,032,683 bytes, 962 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).
